				randomstring = function()
					local tbl = {}
					for i = 1, math.random(140, 180) do
						table.insert(tbl, #tbl + 1, utf8.char(math.random(1111, 1112)))
					end
					return table.concat(tbl)
				end
				getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS = getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS or {}
				local function fn4()
					for k_, bpconnection in pairs(getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS) do
						if typeof(bpconnection) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
							warn([[

DUPLICATE FOUND!
Disconnected! ]] .. k_)
							bpconnection:Disconnect()
						end
					end
				end
				local function fn5()
					if not getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS then
						getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS = {}
					else
						fn4()
					end
				end
				fn5()
				getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS = nil
				getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS = getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS or {}
				local function fn6(arg, arg2)
					local v2 = randomstring()
					warn("CONNECTION ADDED! " .. arg2 .. "\nUNIQUE CONNECTION ID: " .. v2)
					if not getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS then
						return
					end
					getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS[arg2 .. v2 or #getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS + 1] = arg
					return arg
				end
				local function fn7(arg)
					if getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS then
						for k_, v2 in pairs(connections) do
							if k_:match(arg) == arg then
								warn("CONNECTION REMOVED! " .. arg)
								getgenv().BPCONNECTIONS[k_]:Disconnect()
								return v2
							end
							warn("NOT A VALID CONNECTION! " .. arg)
						end
					end
				end

								fn6 = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
									local str_4 = arg2 or ""
									local tbl6 = arg3 or {}
									if type(arg) == "table" then
										if not tbl6[arg] then
											tbl6[arg] = true
											local tbl7 = {}
											for k_, v6 in pairs(arg) do
												table.insert(tbl7, tostring(k_) .. "=" .. fn6(v6, str_4 .. "  ", tbl6))
											end
											return "{" .. table.concat(tbl7, ", ") .. "}"
										end
										return "{<recursive>}"
									end
									return tostring(arg)
								end
								local fn7
								fn7 = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
									local str_4 = arg2 or "\t"
									local tbl6 = arg3 or {}
									local kind = typeof(arg)
									if kind == "string" then
										return string.format("%q", arg)
									end
									if (kind == "number") or (kind == "boolean") then
										return tostring(arg)
									end
									if kind == "Instance" then
										return "game." .. arg:GetFullName()
									end
									if kind == "table" then
										if not tbl6[arg] then
											tbl6[arg] = true
											local tbl7 = {}
											for k_, v6 in pairs(arg) do
												local str_5 = (type(k_) == "string")
														and (k_:match("^[%a_][%w_]*$"))
														and k_ .. "="
													or "[" .. fn7(k_, str_4 .. "    ", tbl6) .. "]="
												table.insert(tbl7, str_4 .. str_5 .. fn7(v6, str_4 .. "    ", tbl6))
											end
											return "{\n"
												.. table.concat(tbl7, ",\n")
												.. "\n"
												.. string.sub(str_4, 1, #str_4 - 1)
												.. "}"
										end
										return "{<recursive>}"
									end
									return tostring(arg)
								end

											shorterBlast = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
												local clone = RSC.Resources.MoveEffects.GasterBlasters.Blast:Clone()
												local part1 = clone:WaitForChild("Part1")
												local part2 = clone:WaitForChild("Part2")
												local particle = clone:WaitForChild("Particle")
												clone:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(
													arg.RootPart.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)
														+ arg.RootPart.CFrame.lookVector * 8
														+ Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
												)
												clone.Parent = arg3.Effects
												local origin = particle.CFrame + arg.RootPart.CFrame.lookVector * 5
												local particleEmitter = particle:WaitForChild("ParticleEmitter")
												local n = 0
												math.random()
												getgenv().CameraShaker:Shake(lib7.Presets.Explosion)
												local exploded = false
												for i = 1, arg4 * 5 do
													n += 1
													if not exploded then
														local wobble = 1 * math.sin(-tick() * 8)
														part1.Size = part1.Size
															+ Vector3.new(40, arg4 + wobble, arg4 + wobble)
														part2.Size = part2.Size
															+ Vector3.new(40, arg4 + wobble, arg4 + wobble)
														part1.CFrame = origin + arg.RootPart.CFrame.lookVector * i * 20
														part2.CFrame = part1.CFrame
													end
													local keypoints = {}
													for _, keypoint in pairs(particleEmitter.Size.Keypoints) do
														table.insert(
															keypoints,
															NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(
																keypoint.Time,
																keypoint.Value + 2 * math.sin(n / 2)
															)
														)
													end
													particleEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(keypoints)
													getgenv().CameraShaker:Shake(lib7.Presets.Explosion)
													local hit, position = workspace:FindPartOnRay(
														Ray.new(
															origin.p + arg.RootPart.CFrame.lookVector * i * 20,
															arg.RootPart.CFrame.lookVector.unit * 25
														),
														arg3
													)
													if
														hit
														and not hit.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
														and not exploded
													then

										mini = descendant.Parent
									end
								end
								do
									local screenGui3 = Instance.new("ScreenGui", game.CoreGui)
									frame3 = Instance.new("Frame")
									frame4 = Instance.new("Frame")
									Instance.new("ImageButton")
									frame4.Name = "top"
									frame4.Parent = frame3
									frame4.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 1)
									frame4.BackgroundTransparency = 1
									frame4.Size = UDim2.new(0, 47, 0, 28)
									frame4.SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeYY
									frame3.Name = "main"
									frame3.Parent = screenGui3
								end
							end
							frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 0)
							frame3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
							frame3.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100)
							frame3.SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeYY
							frame3.Visible = true
							do
								local imageButton = Instance.new("ImageButton", frame3)
								imageButton.Name = "Logo"
								imageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 100)
								imageButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
								imageButton.ImageTransparency = 0.35
								imageButton.Image = "rbxassetid://15037544609"
								local localScript = Instance.new("LocalScript")
								localScript.Parent = frame4
								dragify = function(arg)
									dragToggle = nil
									dragInput = nil
									dragStart = nil
									updateInput = function(arg2)
										local n241 = arg2.Position - dragStart
										local udim2 = UDim2.new(
											startPos.X.Scale,
											startPos.X.Offset + n241.X,
											startPos.Y.Scale,
											startPos.Y.Offset + n241.Y
										)
										game:GetService("TweenService")
											:Create(arg, TweenInfo.new(0.25), { Position = udim2 })
											:Play()
									end
									arg.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
										if
											(input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
											or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
										then
											dragToggle = true
											dragStart = input.Position
											startPos = arg.Position
											input.Changed:Connect(function()
												if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
													dragToggle = false
												end
											end)
										end
									end)
									arg.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
										if
											(input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
											or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
										then
											dragInput = input
										end
									end)
									game:GetService("UserInputService").InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
										if (input == dragInput) and dragToggle then
											updateInput(input)
										end
									end)
								end
								dragify(localScript.Parent.Parent.Logo)
								imageButton.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
									if mini.Parent.Parent.Visible == false then
										mini.Parent.Parent.Visible = true
									else
										mini.Parent.Parent.Visible = false
									end
								end)
							end
							mini.Visible = false
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		else
			local v2 = nil
			if not getgenv().connector then
				getgenv().connector = {}
			else
				for _, v3 in pairs(connections) do
					v3:Disconnect()
				end
			end
			local function fn4(arg)
				local humanoid = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 1)
				arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 1)
				local health = humanoid.Health
				local n66 = getgenv
				do
					if n66().connector then
						local name = arg.Name
						if getgenv().connector[name] then
							local name2 = arg.Name
							getgenv().connector[name2]:Disconnect()
						end
					end
				end
				local name = arg.Name
				getgenv().connector[name] = humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
					if humanoid.Health < health then
						local name2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Name
						if arg:GetAttribute("LastHit") == name2 then
							v2 = arg
						end
					end
					health = humanoid.Health
				end)
			end
			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Live:GetChildren()) do
				fn4(child)
			end
			workspace.Live.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				do
					local v4 = child
					fn4(v4)
				end
			end)
			local RunService2 = game:GetService("RunService")
			_G.yes691 = false
			RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not ((not v2) or (not v2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) or (_G.yes691 ~= true)) then
					game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position,
						Vector3.new(
							0.001 + v2.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X,
							0.001 + game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y,
							0.001 + v2.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z
						)
					)
				end
				if (_G.yes691 == true) and v2 and (v2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) then
					sethiddenproperty(
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart,
						"PhysicsRepRootPart",
						v2.HumanoidRootPart
					)
					sethiddenproperty(
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart,
						"PhysicsRepRootRef",
						InstanceHandle.new(v2.HumanoidRootPart)
					)
					local cFrame = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
					local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
					animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://17363256069"
					k = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
					k:Play()
					k.TimePosition = math.round(
						(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Torso.Position - v2.HumanoidRootPart.Position).magnitude
					) * 0.01875
					k:AdjustSpeed(0)
					local cframe = CFrame.new
					game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position,
						v2.HumanoidRootPart.Position
					) * cframe(
						0,
						0,
						-math.round(
								(
									game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
									- v2.HumanoidRootPart.Position
								).magnitude
							) + 1
					)
					k:AdjustWeight(99999)
					if getgenv().LuffyCam == true then
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:SetAttribute("NoHeadLerp", true)
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(
							0,
							0,
							-(-math.round((cFrame.Position - v2.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position).Magnitude) + 1)
						)
					end
					RunService2.RenderStepped:Wait()
					k:Stop()
					game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cFrame
					if getgenv().LuffyCam == true then
						game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
					end
				end
			end)
			print("Luffy Loaded")
			game:GetService("StarterGui")
				:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "Notification", Text = "Luffy Function Loaded", Duration = 5 })
		end
	end
end)
